class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root
        
        
    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            # this is a leaf node:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs+1], True, None, None, None, None)
        
        next_level = level // 2
        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - next_level, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - next_level, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        
        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                new_node = Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
            else:
                new_node = Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
            else:
                new_node = Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
            
        return new_node

```python
class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root
        
        
    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            # this is a leaf node:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs+1], True, None, None, None, None)
        
        next_level = level // 2
        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - next_level, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - next_level, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        
        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                new_node = Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
            else:
                new_node = Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
            else:
                new_node = Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
            
        return new_node
```

Reason in a repair-and-recheck way.

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        if level >= 1:
            return self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return None

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs], True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        if level >= 1:
            return self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return None

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs], True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        if level >= 1:
            return self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return None

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs], True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        if level >= 1:
            return self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return None

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

```
class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        def helper(grid, r, c, size):
            if size == 1:
                return Node(grid[r][c] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)
            half = size // 2
            topLeft = helper(grid, r, c, half)
            topRight = helper(grid, r, c + half, half)
            bottomLeft = helper(grid, r + half, c, half)
            bottomRight = helper(grid, r + half, c + half, half)
            if topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val:
                return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)
            return Node(False, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root
        
    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs], True, None, None, None, None)
        
        next_level = level // 2
        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - next_level, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - next_level, cs + next_level, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs + next_level, ce, next_level)
        
        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
        
        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```
This uses `re - next_level` and `ce - next_level` as original, and `grid[rs][cs]` not `==1`. It fixes tr/br and else. It is closer. Should we use `grid[rs][cs]` or `grid[rs][cs] == 1`? ```
```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return root

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        if level >= 1:
            return self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return None

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        if level >= 1:
            return self.buildTree(grid, 0, level, 0, level, level)
        return None

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        next_level = level // 2
        mid_r = rs + next_level
        mid_c = cs + next_level

        tl = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        tr = self.buildTree(grid, rs, mid_r, mid_c, ce, next_level)
        bl = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, cs, mid_c, next_level)
        br = self.buildTree(grid, mid_r, re, mid_c, ce, next_level)

        if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf:
            if tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
